公司簡介
China Modern Dairy Holdings Ltd. (the “Company” or “Modern            中 國 現 代 牧 業 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」或「現 代 牧
Dairy”, stock code: 1117.HK) and its subsidiaries (collectively the   業」，股份代號：1117.HK）及其附屬公司（合稱「本
“Group”) is a leading dairy cows farming operator and raw milk        集團」 ）是國內乳牛畜牧營運商及原料奶生產商的翹
producer in the Chinese mainland. We invest in and operate 45         楚。我們在國內投資及營運45個牧場公司，擁有53
farming companies in the Chinese mainland, with 53 modern             個現代化牧場，覆蓋14個省份、自治區及直轄市。
dairy farms, spanning over 14 provinces, autonomous regions           飼養逾46萬頭乳牛，年化產奶量達350萬噸以上。
and municipalities. We raise over 460,000 dairy cows with an          本公司首創「種養加一體化、零距離2小時」，是國
annualized milk production exceeding 3.50 million tons. The           內首家採用一體化生產模式並獲得SGS認證的頭部
Company pioneered the “integrated planting, breeding, and             企業。現代牧業品牌純牛奶已連續十三年，榮獲被
processing, zero-distance 2-hour” integrated production model         譽為食品業的「諾貝爾獎」世界食品品質評鑑大會金
and was the first leading enterprise in the Chinese mainland to       獎，是品質與營養的肯定。近年，我們的業務板塊
adopt such model and obtain SGS certification. Modern Dairy           更擴展至種業研發、牧草種植、飼料、奶牛養殖、
branded pure milk has been awarded the Gold Medal at the              產業鏈數智平台及沙漠有機奶業務等。
Monde Selection – widely regarded as the “Nobel Prize of the
food industry” – for thirteen consecutive years, in recognition
of its outstanding quality and nutritional value. In recent years,
our business segments have also expanded to breeding research
and development, forage plantation, feed, dairy cows breeding,
value chain digital intelligence platform and desert organic milk
business as well.

    INDUSTRY OVERVIEW                                                              行業概覽

    In the first half of 2026, China’s dairy industry demonstrated resilience      2026年上半年，中國乳製品行業在複雜的市場環境
    and structural changes in a complex market environment, and                    中展現出韌性與結構性變化，行業正處於加速整合
    the industry was at a critical period of accelerated integration and           與轉型升級的關鍵時期。
    transformation and upgrading.

    In the first half of the year, although the national average price of raw      上半年，儘管全國原料奶平均價格延續了近年的下
    milk continued the downward trend in recent years, market supply and           行趨勢，但市場供需已出現積極的自我調節跡象。
    demand have shown positive signs of self-regulation. With the recovery         隨著國內大包粉價格回升（至6月份達約2.7 – 2.8萬
    of domestic large-package milk powder prices (reaching approximately           元╱噸）及政府補貼政策的落地，大型乳企收奶及
    RMB27,000-28,000/ton by June) and the implementation of government-            噴粉意願顯著增強，有效防止了低價奶源流入市場
    subsidy policies, large dairy companies have significantly increased their     引發無序競爭。這一大型乳企的戰略性收奶舉措，
    willingness to collect raw milk and spray powder, effectively preventing       帶動了近期散奶價格強勢反彈，並一度突破合同奶
    low-priced milk sources from entering the market and triggering                價。此外，國內牛肉價格亦呈現持續回升態勢，為
    disorderly competition. Such strategic raw-milk-collecting move by             牧場優化牛群結構提供了有利條件。產能方面，儘
    large dairy companies has led to a strong rebound in the price of bulk         管國內奶牛存欄量持續下降，但得益於奶牛單產水
    milk recently, which once even exceeded the contract milk price. In            平的不斷提升，全國原料奶產量保持穩健增長。據
    addition, domestic beef prices have also continued to rebound, providing       國家統計局數據，2026年上半年全國原料奶產量達
    favorable conditions for farms to optimize their herd structure. In terms      1,909萬噸，同比增長2.4%。
    of production capacity, although the number of domestic dairy cows
    continues to decline, national raw milk production has maintained steady
    growth thanks to the continuous improvement of dairy cow yields.
    According to data from the National Bureau of Statistics, China’s raw milk
    production reached 19.09 million tons in the first half of 2026, a year-on-
    year increase of 2.4%.

    half of 2026, up 6.0% year-on-year. According to Chinese customs               據中國海關數據顯示，期內中國各類乳製品進口量
    data, China’s import volume of various dairy products during the period        達到141.25萬噸，同比增加2.2%，延續去年增長的
    reached 1.4125 million tons, a year-on-year increase of 2.2%, continuing       趨勢。
    last year’s growth trend.

                                                     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
                                                                                            管理層討論及分析


In the face of periodic challenges in the industry, the state has intensively   面對行業階段性挑戰，國家密集出台多項專項紓困
introduced a number of special relief and support policies. The Ministry of     與扶持政策。農業農村部聯合金融機構建立奶牛養
Agriculture and Rural Affairs has joined forces with financial institutions     殖企業「白名單」制度，精準提供貸款貼息與保險支
to establish a “white list” system for dairy farming enterprises to             持；同時，持續推進優質基礎母牛擴群提質及「糧改
accurately provide loan discounts and insurance support; at the same            飼」項目以降低飼草成本。政策亦明確鼓勵大型乳企
time, it continued to promote the expansion and quality improvement of          發揮產業鏈帶動作用，通過訂單收購、金融擔保等
high-quality basic cows and the “grain-to-feed” policy to reduce forage         方式與上游牧場構建利益共同體。宏觀規劃層面，
costs. These policies also explicitly encourage large dairy companies to        隨著《加快農業農村現代化「十五五」規劃》的印發，
play a leading role in the industry chain and build a community of shared       中國乳業迎來「由大到強」的關鍵轉折。政策推動行
interests with upstream dairy farms through order acquisition, financial        業加速洗牌與規模化進程，提速產品的國產替代；
guarantees and other means. At the macro planning level, with the               並鼓勵奶酪、乳清等高附加值產品的發展，為乳企
issuance of the “15th Five-Year Plan for Accelerating the Modernization         優化產品結構、拓寬盈利空間指明了方向。
of Agriculture and Rural Areas”, China’s dairy industry has ushered in a
key turning point “from large to strong”. These policies have accelerated
the industry’s reshuffling and scaling-up process, expedited the domestic
substitution of products, and encouraged the development of high value-
added products such as cheese and whey, pointing the way for dairy
companies to optimize their product structure and expand their profit
margins.


BUSINESS OVERVIEW                                                               業務概述

Our Group is a leading enterprise in China’s dairy cow farming and raw          本集團是中國乳牛畜牧與原料奶生產的領軍企業，
milk production, committed to providing high-quality raw milk to our            致力於為合作夥伴提供優質原料奶，用於加工各類
partners for processing into various premium dairy products. We have            高端乳製品。我們與頂尖乳企締結了長期穩固的戰
established long-term, stable strategic cooperative relationships with top-     略合作關係，實現優勢互補，共同推動中國乳業的
tier dairy enterprises, achieving complementary advantages and jointly          高質量發展。

     SHARE AWARD SCHEMES                                                            股份獎勵計劃

     “Share Award Scheme”) which shall remain effective for ten years.              勵計劃」），股份獎勵計劃將維持十年有效。股份獎
     The goal of the share award schemes are to motivate the employees to           勵計劃旨在激勵僱員為本公司創造最大價值及與本
     maximize the value of and share the results with the Company in order to       公司僱員分享成果，以令本集團實現可持續發展。
     achieve sustainable development of the Group.


     (a) The purpose of the Share Award Scheme                                      (a) 股份獎勵計劃的目的

          The purpose of the Share Award Scheme is to recognise and                   股份獎勵計劃旨在表揚及激勵本集團若干管理
          motivate the contribution of certain members of management of               層成員的貢獻，提供獎勵及協助本集團挽留其
          the Group and to provide incentives and help the Group in retaining         現有管理層成員，並為彼等提供實現本集團長
          its existing members of management and to provide them with                 期業務目標的直接經濟利益。
          a direct economic interest in attaining the long-term business
          objectives of the Group.

     3. CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                            3. 會計政策變動

         The IASB has issued a number of amendments to IFRS Accounting              國際會計準則理事會發佈了多項對國際財務報
         Standards that are first effective for the current accounting period.      告準則會計準則的修訂，該等修訂於當期會計
         Of these, only the amendments to IFRS 9, Financial instruments             期間首次生效。其中，僅國際財務報告準則第
         and IFRS 7, Financial instruments: Disclosures – Amendments to the         9號金融工具及國際財務報告準則第7號金融工
         classification and measurement of financial instruments, are relevant      具：披露 – 金融工具分類及計量的修訂的修訂
         to the Group’s financial statements. The impacts of adopting these         與本集團的財務報表相關。以下將討論採納該
         amendments are discussed below.                                            等修訂的影響。


         The Group has not applied any new standard or interpretation that          本集團並無應用任何於本會計期間尚未生效的
         is not yet effective for the current accounting period.                    新訂準則或詮釋。

         Amendments to IFRS 9, Financial instruments and IFRS 7, Financial         國際財務報告準則第9號金融工具及國際財務
         instruments: Disclosures – Amendments to the classification and           報告準則第7號金融工具：披露 – 金融工具分
         measurement of financial instruments                                      類及計量的修訂 的修訂

         The amendments cover three main aspects:                                   該等修訂涵蓋三個大方面：

         •    The amendments clarify when a financial asset or a financial          •   該等修訂明確了金融資產或金融負債的確
              liability is recognised and derecognised. They also introduce             認及終止確認時間。此外，亦引入了一項
              an optional exception that permits an entity to derecognise a             可選例外情況，允許實體在滿足特定條件
              financial liability before the settlement date when the financial         的情況下，在金融負債透過電子支付系統
              liability is settled in cash using an electronic payment system,          以現金結算時，於結算日之前終止確認金
              provided that specific criteria are met.                                  融負債。

         •    For the assessment of whether a financial asset has contractual       •   為評估金融資產是否具有僅支付本金及未
              cash flows that are solely payments of principal and interest             償還本金利息的合約現金流量，該等修訂
              on the principal amount outstanding, the amendments                       明確了利息的評估，並為具有或然特徵的
              clarify the assessment of interest and introduce an additional            金融資產引入了一項附加測試，例如與環
              test for the financial assets with contingent features, for               境、社會或管治（
                                                                                               「ESG」
                                                                                                   ）相關的特徵。該
              example environmental, social or governance (“ESG”) linked                等修訂亦明確了具有無追索權特徵的金融
              features. The amendments also clarify the difference between              資產與合約關聯工具之間的區別，這可能
              financial assets with non-recourse features and contractually             會改變適用的評估。
              linked instruments which may then change the applicable
              assessments.

         •    The amendments introduce new disclosures for disposals of             •   該等修訂引入了新的披露要求，用於披露
              investments in equity instruments designated at FVOCI, and                指定為按公平值計入其他全面收入的權益
              for financial instruments not measured at FVPL which contain              工具的投資出售，以及未按公平值計入損
              contractual terms that could change the amount of contractual             益計量且其包含可能因與基本貸款風險及
              cash flows based on the occurrence or non-occurrence of a                 成本變化無直接關係的或然事件的發生或
              contingent event that does not relate directly to changes in              不發生而改變合約現金流量金額的金融工
              basic lending risks and costs.                                            具。

     8. INCOME TAX (Continued)                                                   8. 所得稅（續）

         The Company is incorporated as an exempted company and as such            本公司作為獲豁免公司註冊成立，因此不受開
         is not subject to any tax in the Cayman Islands.                          曼群島稅項的約束。

         No provision for taxation in Hong Kong has been made as the               並無就香港稅項作出撥備，原因為本集團於香
         Group’s income neither arises in, nor is derived from Hong Kong.          港並無產生收入亦無源自香港之收入。

         Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”)               根 據 中 華 人 民 共 和 國（「中 國」）企 業 所 得 稅 法
         on Enterprise Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation              （
                                                                                  「企業所得稅法」      ）及企業所得稅法實施條例，
         Regulation of the EIT Law, the statutory income tax rate of the PRC       中國附屬公司於兩個期間的法定所得稅稅率為
         subsidiaries is 25% for both periods.                                     25%。

         Taxation arising in other jurisdictions is calculated at the rates        其他司法權區產生的稅項乃根據有關司法權區
         prevailing in the relevant jurisdictions.                                 現行稅率計算。

         According to the prevailing tax rules and regulation of the EIT Law,      根據企業所得稅法現行稅務規則及法規，截至
         52 subsidiaries of the Group is exempted from EIT for taxable profit      2026年6月30日止六個月，本集團的52間附屬
         from the operation of agricultural business in the PRC for the six        公司（截至2025年6月30日止六個月：58間）於
         months ended 30 June 2026 (six months ended 30 June 2025: 58).            中國經營農業業務產生的應課稅溢利獲豁免繳
                                                                                   納企業所得稅。

         Pursuant to the PRC EIT Law, a 10% withholding tax is levied on           根據中國企業所得稅法，於中國內地成立的外
         dividends declared to foreign investors from the foreign investment       資 企 業 向 外 商 投 資 者 宣 派 股 息 須 繳 納 10%的
         enterprises established in Chinese mainland. The requirement is           預扣稅。該規定自2008年1月1日起生效，並
         effective from 1 January 2008 and applies to earnings after 31            適用於2007年12月31日後的盈利。倘中國與
         December 2007. A lower withholding tax rate may be applied                外商投資者所在司法權區訂立了稅項條約，則
         if there is a tax treaty between China and the jurisdiction of the        可調低預扣稅稅率。本集團的適用比率為5%

         termination date, if the spot rate is equal to or less than the cap strike            期，當即期匯率等於或低於約定的上限匯率

                                                                                              「上限匯率」     ），且等於或高於下限匯率（            「下限

         Floor Rate, the Company will exchange RMB for USD at an exchange                      本公司將以（6.478+（即期匯率 – 下限匯率）             ）的

              Perpetual capital securities issued by the Group are classified as         當滿足以下兩個條件時，本集團發行的永
              equity instruments when both of the following conditions are               續資本證券將被歸類為權益工具：
              satisfied:

              (i)    The perpetual capital securities have no contractual                (i)    永續資本證券無合約義務以現金或
                     obligation to pay in cash or other financial assets to other               其他金融資產向其他方支付款項，
                     parties nor to exchange financial assets or liabilities under              亦無義務在潛在不利情況下與其他
                     potential adverse condition with other parties;                            方交換金融資產或負債；

              (ii)   If the perpetual capital securities will be or may be settled       (ii)   如果永續資本證券將或可能以發行
                     in the issuer’s own equity instruments, and it is a non-                   人自身的權益工具進行結算，且其
                     derivative that includes no contractual obligation for                     為非衍生性工具，不包含發行人交
                     the issuer to deliver a variable number of its own equity                  付可變數目自身權益工具的合約義
                     instruments.                                                               務。

              The interest expenses or dividends of perpetual capital                    被歸類為權益工具的永續資本證券的利息
              securities classified as equity instruments are treated as profit          支出或股息，被視為本集團的溢利分配。
              distribution of the Group. The repurchase or cancellation of               購回或註銷該等工具被視為權益變動。相
              these instruments is treated as a change in equity. The related            關交易成本從權益中扣除。
              transaction costs are deducted from equity.

              On 10 April 2026, Modern Farming (Group) Co., Ltd. (the                    於2026年4月10日，本公司非全資附
              “Issuer”), a non-wholly owned subsidiary of the Company                    屬 公 司 現 代 牧 業（集 團）有 限 公 司（「發 行
              issued RMB500,000,000 perpetual capital securities with                    人」 ）發行了人民幣500,000,000元的永續
              the aggregate net proceeds (after transaction cost of                      資 本 證 券 ， 所 得 款 項 淨 額 總 計（經 扣 除
              RMB2,950,000) of RMB497,050,000.                                           交易成本人民幣2,950,000元）為人民幣
                                                                                         497,050,000元。

              The Issuer used the proceeds from the perpetual capital                    發行人將永續資本證券的所得款項用於企
              securities for corporate operating purposes. Details of the                業營運目的。永續資本證券的條款詳情於
              terms of the perpetual capital securities are disclosed in the             本公司日期為2026年3月24日的公告中披
              Company’s announcement dated 24 March 2026.                                露。

              The Group made some important judgments in applying above                  本集團於應用上述會計政策時作出了一
              accounting policies, which have significant influences on the              些重要判斷，該等判斷對財務報表的金
              amount of financial statements. The issued perpetual capital               額產生了重大影響。所發行的永續資本證
              securities has no maturity date. Since the Group has the right             券並無到期日。由於本集團有權延期支付
              to defer interest payment and the option for redemption of                 利息，並有權選擇贖回永續資本證券。本
              perpetual capital securities. The Group has no obligation to               集團無義務向其他實體交付現金或其他金
              deliver cash or another financial asset to another entity, or to           融資產，亦無義務在可能對發行人不利的
              exchange financial assets or financial liabilities with another            情況下與其他實體交換金融資產或金融負
              entity under conditions that are potentially unfavorable to the            債，永續資本證券被歸類為權益工具，符
